**Selection and scope:** Fresh independent review of [Part 2 #463](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/463), using the approved [UI plan](research-part-2-ui-plan.md), scoped specification, and delivery outcomes. No intervening commits were listed. Accepted backend `2a048dd7`, Part 1, historical approvals, generated data, and unrelated concurrent changes remain outside the revision.

#### Standards

**S1 — P3 maintainability judgment: repetitive source statuses obscure useful conditions.** `components/world-7/research/researchSources.tsx:63–69` always prints intrinsic and applied rewards, acquired/active flags, and optional companion or Research-level facts. Actual Point sources repeat active Rift Stalker's +10 reward and applied 10 Points. Shape milestones repeat the level in the name, Condition, and Required Research level, followed by acquired/active flags. Under `CODING_STANDARDS.md` SIMPLE-01 and the possible Duplicated Code/clarity smell, remove repeated disclosures when the existing gate, value, and opacity convey the same facts. Preserve genuinely distinct reward/application amounts, companion activation conditions, caps, and milestone context. This is a judgment, not a hard standards violation or incorrect calculation. Richer Part 2 conditions do not require exact Part 1 formatting, a new abstraction, or a Part 1 migration. No other actionable YAGNI/DRY or impossible-undefined finding was established. The shared scroll hook serves current consumers; optional state checks reflect genuinely optional data.

#### Spec

**R1 — P2: source tooltips do not match the required concise Part 1 treatment.** `components/world-7/research/researchSources.tsx:62–69` expands each reward into separate name, intrinsic, applied, acquired/active, and repeated condition/status lines. The UI plan at line 54 says **“Match the four Part 1 examples”**; the specification at line 76 carries the concise value-attached source pattern and intrinsic inactive rewards through opacity. Part 1's `researchContent.tsx:43–57` uses `Source: value`, with an obtainable intrinsic reward when inactive. The new code correctly retains intrinsic inactive rewards and opacity, but adds repetitive status prose. Actual `tip-points-focus.png` and `tip-owned-shapes-focus-segment-3.png` show the mismatch. Players must scroll past repeated facts to compare contributions and reach lower conditions. The approved preview already has this issue; matching it does not waive the explicit reference requirement. Use concise source/value lines, applied values when active, and intrinsic rewards with opacity when inactive. Keep distinct applied amounts and necessary conditions without repeated statuses or levels.

### Part 2 Phase 8 independent UI review — 2026-10-14

**Authority and method:** Fresh independent review under the delegated supervisor's Phase 8 selection and scoped review request. Started with UI patterns and used code-review, Impeccable context, coding standards, contribution guidance, and domain vocabulary guidance. Standards and Spec reviewers ran separately and serially. The parent inspected code, prepared/rendered evidence, retained assertions, actual screenshots, and the approved preview. This was a scoped desktop review, not a whole-site redesign or a fresh application run. Plan approval did not exempt the plan or implementation from standards.

**Fixed point and scope:** `HEAD` and accepted backend both resolve to `2a048dd7ee17e24a3f5bb2441c0b66d89dd4646c`; no selected commits follow that baseline. Reviewed `git diff 2a048dd7ee17e24a3f5bb2441c0b66d89dd4646c -- components/world-7/research/researchContent.tsx data/domain/world-7/research.tsx`, plus the complete untracked `researchGrid.tsx`, `researchShapes.tsx`, and `researchSources.tsx`. Included the standalone UI plan and current handoff. Read published [#463](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/463), all six delivery outcomes, shared row/image/value contracts, and relevant accepted Research owner APIs. Excluded concurrent Hole, Spelunking, navigation, tests, and human-generated data changes.

#### Standards and maintainability

1. **S1 — P3 judgment: Masterclass adds internal reduction representations without useful context.** `components/world-7/research/researchGrid.tsx:61,63` adds **Accumulated reduction bonus: 4.23K%** and **Current cost factor: ×0.02**. The same row already gives the static **Total Reduction:-97.68%**, **Daily reduction: 30%**, and **Accumulated current reduction: 97.7%**. The approved `linked-masterclass.png` has neither extra field. The plan at lines 63 and 76 limits secondary results to clarifying linked effects and selects daily/effective accumulated reduction. These extra fields expose the backend's inverse-denominator input and another rounded representation without explaining their relationship. This is a [SIMPLE-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#simple-01--solve-the-current-problem) / [LANG-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#lang-01--simplified-technical-english-ste) clarity and necessity judgment, not a wrong calculation or hard standards breach. Remove the two extra fields to restore the approved simpler row. Keep affected classes, Research grant, daily reduction, and effective accumulated reduction.

The retained numeric record supplies accumulator **4223.20000000001**, effective reduction **97.68**, and cost factor **0.0231310140636565**. `research.tsx:173,214–216` establishes their distinct meanings; React does not calculate them. Evidence: actual `linked-masterclass-150.png` and `research-p2-ui-production-numeric.json` versus approved `linked-masterclass.png`, in the production and preview runs linked below. The Standards delegate found no documented breach. The Spec delegate judged these extra fields a minor fidelity difference, not another blocker; S1 is the parent's maintainability finding for decision.

No new impossible-absence defense was found. `activeOwner`, `linkedState`, placement, contributor cap, and gate are genuinely optional; initialized balances, ownership, and collections use direct access. No UI-owned Point, prerequisite, rotation, coverage, or effect formula was introduced. The image accessor uses existing Shape metadata. Small duplicated separator cleanup and Posty Note label normalization are observations, not a demand for a new abstraction or a separate finding. The shared source renderer serves two current lists. No broader YAGNI, framework, new description, or generated-edit defect was established.

#### Spec and visual fidelity

1. **R1 — P2: Active Shape references render at board-sized dimensions instead of preview thumbnail size.** `components/world-7/research/researchGrid.tsx:105` passes `shape.getImageData()` to `IconImage` without a `scale`. The shared primitive defaults to **1**. Golden Rectangle therefore renders **111×83 px** inside upgrade details. The approved Masterclass preview uses a compact thumbnail; the actual row has a large artwork block that moves factors and linked conditions apart. The same factor reference repeats on all **13** Golden Rectangle-owned upgrades. This breaks the compact DataRow/detail composition selected at UI-plan lines 17–18 and 41; correct source dimensions do not require full-size display. Use bounded, aspect-preserving thumbnail scaling for active Shape references. Keep owning metadata unchanged.

Concrete comparison: production `linked-masterclass-150.png` and crown rows versus preview `linked-masterclass.png` and `linked-crowns.png`. Production `research-p2-ui-production-imagery.json` records `ResShape4`, natural and rendered **111×83**, `scale: 1`, including owner rows **129–132**. `IconImage.tsx:9,18–23` explains the rendering mechanism. This is a fidelity defect, not missing artwork or incorrect saved ownership.

All six outcomes have corresponding UI and supplied real-save evidence: Points and sources; all 60 searchable upgrades; levels, caps, effects, orthogonal access and separate prerequisites; Research-linked conditions; ten Shape definitions with ownership and actual placement/rotation; and complete single-owner reverse coverage. Cooking retains the accepted Rift 61 text/code-state distinction. Divine Design keeps its accepted source text and separate grants/contribution. No board, categories, planner, purchase/placement controls, activity panel, or cross-domain navigation was added.

Rubik, charcoal surfaces, centered shared tabs, DataRow borders/gaps, text-first upgrade identity, and grouped Point context match the selected direction. Source-wrapper spacing, slightly different Shape tracks, access information icons, and explicit linked labels remain minor composition differences. Actual and preview first viewports and linked Sigils, Cooking, boats, crowns, trades, Masterclass, and Divine rows were inspected. Complete actual Shapes and individual unowned rows retain descriptions without hypothetical placements. Part 1 screenshots and the narrow tracked diff support preservation.

### Part 2 Phase 6 UI plan and rendered preview — 2026-10-14

**Selection:** The delegated supervisor selected fresh Phase 6 for human-selected approved Part 2 under [the feature workflow](../human/feature-workflow.md#6-plan-the-ui-and-rendered-preview). **Standalone deliverable:** [Research Part 2 UI plan](research-part-2-ui-plan.md). Preserve Part 1's summaries, default Observations, four existing tabs, and conventions. Append Grid and Shapes to the same ContentTabs. Use a full-width flat DataRow catalogue, with every upgrade visible by default and name/effect search. Keep Points and all supported sources beside the catalogue. Show ten illustrated Shape definitions with actual ownership, position, rotation, current factor, and complete active coverage references. No optional filter, invented category, board, planner, activity panel, or gameplay control is needed.

**Information sufficiency for all six outcomes:**

| Approved outcome | Prepared information used by the preview |
|---|---|
| Point balance and sources | `points.earned`, `spent`, `available`, all contributor values/caps/conditions, and `pointMilestone` cumulative reward context. Actual result: 115 / 115 / 0. |
| Complete searchable catalogue | `upgrades` supplies 60 non-placeholder records, generated names, and populated descriptions. Empty search keeps unpurchased and maxed entries. |
| Current effects and access | Each upgrade supplies purchased level, cap, current primary/secondary description values, reachability, shared factor, active owner, Shape factor, prerequisites, and linked state. No UI arithmetic determines these facts. |
| Linked unlocks and contributors | Prepared Sigil Lore, Cooking Mastery, qualifying boats, reclaimed crowns, complete Glimbo blocks, original three Masterclasses, and separate Divine Design conditions appear beside their owners. ### Part 2 Phase 5 independent backend review — 2026-10-14

**Authority and method:** Fresh independent review under the delegated supervisor's Phase 5 selection and review request, within existing human delegation. This was not a verbatim human review request. Used `code-review`, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, domain vocabulary guidance, backend patterns, and domain testing guidance. #### Standards

1. **S1 — P2: New square parsing remains on the parent.** `data/domain/world-7/research.tsx:969–972` translates the saved owner and assigns `activeOwner` and `shapeBonusPercent` directly on every square. [MODEL-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#model-01--model-responsible-concepts) requires aspect parsing and update logic on the responsible class. The plan also gives `ResearchGridBonus` ownership of purchased state and effect inputs. Behavior can be correct while this ownership breach remains. Move the new square-state translation into a `ResearchGridBonus` parsing method and have the parent call it. This does not require migrating unrelated legacy parsing.
2. **S2 — P3: Reachability tests depend on implementation spelling.** `tests/domains/research/grid-catalogue.test.ts:221–224` asserts literal source strings for the column constant and neighbor `push` statements. These assertions fail after an equivalent refactor and do not prove the unobserved row-edge transition. The [specification's application seam](research-spec-draft.md#testing-decisions) requires public results, not object layout or isolated formula copies; domain testing guidance likewise favors calculation results. Keep the extracted selection-mask and public reachability comparisons. Record unobserved row-edge behavior and its source review in the handoff instead of locking tests to source spelling. Do not synthesize a save to fill that gap. This is a test-maintainability finding, not an observed numerical defect.

S1 came from the independent Standards reviewer and the parent's owner review. S2 came from the parent's test-seam review. No other grounded YAGNI, DRY, speculative abstraction, impossible-required-object defense, or frontend-overfitting finding was established. Missing saved fields, absent placements, unowned squares, and upgrades without linked state are genuine absence cases. The minimal Glimbo owner and narrow Cooking/Sailing predicates serve current requirements. No layout, invented upgrade category, planner, or activity panel was added.

#### Spec

**No grounded finding.** The selected backend supplies all six approved outcomes: earned/spent/available Points and sources; 60 real upgrades with all 240 indexed positions retained; purchased state, caps, effects, and separate requirements; linked conditions; ten Shape definitions; and saved single-owner reverse coverage.

Concrete mechanics match the inspected source boundaries. `research.tsx:1194–1214` uses the existing milestone result, floors earned Points, rounds the running spending sum, and leaves available Points unclamped. `research.tsx:1262–1274` consumes parsed Gaming crowns and Glimbo totals before early numerical Grid preparation. `research.tsx:1608–1624` consumes settled owner values during late presentation. {

The underscores and punctuation above are part of the source strings. These quotations do not rewrite the exported description. The existing template also retains its punctuation.

**What already has numeric substitution rules:** The game replaces the following tokens at `../IdleonWikiBot/codefiles/idleon1.22.txt:285043–285053`. The static templates and the inputs for these calculations are already available. This is not a missing numeric-text export.

| Token | Existing source substitution |
|---|---|
| `{` | Current primary Grid bonus, rounded and formatted for display; Divine Design uses it for Drop Rate. |
| `}` | Primary multiplier, `1 + primary bonus / 100`. |
| `$` | Current secondary Grid bonus. |
| `^` | Secondary multiplier, `1 + secondary bonus / 100`. |
| `&` | Accumulated reduction from account option 499, using the game's reduction calculation. |
| `\|` | Rounded purchased level. |

These numeric rules already operate in the supplied game source. Part 2 website description formatting remains planned, not implemented or runtime-verified here. The `<` replacement uses only the two permitted source strings, not another numeric formula.

**Planned website behavior:** Keep both strings beside Divine Design's owning `ResearchGridBonus` description-selection behavior. Limit replacement to Grid index 173. Select the King Doot sentence only when companion helper 0 equals `1`; otherwise select the permanent-Arctis sentence. Preserve source underscores, string punctuation, and the existing template punctuation through substitution. Use existing description display formatting. Then substitute the separately calculated current numeric bonus into `{`. This branch does not inspect a character's selected Divinity god. Keep text selection separate from the actual Arctis grant and conditional shared Grid multiplier calculations.

Other levels use the selected RGB entry's hue and saturation strength `5 × (maximum channel − minimum channel) / maximum channel`, then `sepia(1) saturate(strength) hue-rotate(hue − 40deg) brightness(1.3)`. Levels ten and above retain the final reference hue without excess-level darkening. CSS filters produce palette-derived presentation, not exact RGB replacement. Original sprites, CDN paths, and **36px** dimensions remain. This is a deliberate website choice, not game-image parity. Actual rendered found levels span **3–11** and **14**. ### Observation source decision — 2026-10-14

- **Discovery versus study:** Versioned `../IdleonWikiBot/codefiles/idleon1.22.txt:309977-309981` confirms Reef index 30 and generated study level 72. The revealed prefix reaches 34 entries at Research level 70 (`:242540-242551`). In-map actor creation uses that prefix without the study check (`:293794-293820`). Actor 672's release handler rolls against row field 6, Reef's 101, without checking row field 1 (`:269925-269960,270015-270033`). Actor 741 checks row field 1 for lens assignment (`:284940-284959`); its hint explicitly calls this the study level (`:285205-285287`). Keep the generated record and all numerical formulas. Correct the public semantic contract for every Observation.
- **Palette:** Actor 741's Insight bar uses the next level's exact 11-entry RGB palette, then subtracts eight per level beyond palette index ten, flooring each channel at zero (`:285213-285236`). This colors only the progress fill. Current level text and game artwork remain unchanged.
- **Inactive rewards:** Reuse intrinsic Sushi/Jelly preview getters, companion metadata and actual activation state, Grid base/mode/rank information, and existing Research Event Shop scales. Event Shop 33/34/35/36 grant +1 Kaleidoscope, +1 Magnifier, +3 attempts/day, and +1 Shape. Late Jelly EXP rewards remain ×1 effective while inactive but offer ×1.4, ×1.3, and ×1.6. Display metadata must not alter those current totals or split grouped companion factors incorrectly.
- **Other real inactive sources:** Existing producer metadata also supplies Prehistoric's capped +50% Research EXP, Paper Hardhat's +2% per purchased level, Pen N Paper's bare ×1.01 per Fountain level before Marbleization, Brainhead's +1 Magnifying Glass, and future Posty Shape/capacity rewards. These are intrinsic rewards, not current totals or guessed maxima. Research Outpost differs: its selector is already available but another category is selected. Royal Armory exposes the Research category's factor for the current built-Outpost count and its actual selection state. Native selector availability uses Compounding Outposting, not the Support/Savage map-type unlocks (`idleon1.22.txt:289751-289775,290237-290253`; `docs/game-snippets/equinox/RoyalG.1.22.js:138-149`). No numerical producer defect was changed.
- **Evidence boundary:** The complete private evidence artifact records exact original rows, caller references, palette values, producer APIs, and retained-save examples. Read-only original runtime corroborates versioned 1.22 source. Current live release version remains independently unconfirmed. No Reef click, RNG roll, map switch, lens assignment, companion activation, or save mutation was exercised. The retained real save is not a newly extracted live save/result pair.

### Compact Research UI refinement — 2026-10-14

- **Authority:** The human asked to remedy the four prioritized findings in the fresh independent UX critique and show the result. They explicitly permitted progressive disclosure for supporting ledgers while preserving every entity and all supported data. This scope supersedes earlier no-disclosure constraints, not the accepted backend or Part 2 boundary.
- **Design choices:** Preserve Rubik, charcoal surfaces, shared DataRow shells, labels, game artwork, and canonical compact formatting. Put current rates, attempts, offline percentage, and progress in the overview. Add theme-blue same-page section links, per-section Overview links, and return links after long lists rather than tabs or an alternate view. Use two overview columns at narrow width. Keep undiscovered hints and all independent discovery conditions visible; put discovered-entry audit conditions in row details. Emphasize discovered/undiscovered and reached/upcoming state locally, without dimming whole locked rows or implying map access.
- **Disclosure pattern:** Reuse native `details` / `summary`, as in Gaming Import details, Snail/Crown chances, and Royal Armory Alternatives. Source-save provenance, discovery sources, production factors, lens ownership sources, Observation relationships/contributors, offline sources, Shape sources, and note context have explicit labels. Compact values with information icons also open native exact-value details by click or keyboard. No new component framework, state persistence, game formulas, or libraries were added.
- **Language:** Introduce all three lens roles beside production. Use up/down/left/right and before cap in player-facing guidance. Keep mathematical operations, supplied gates, numeric lens/character identities, source inputs, caps, named companion factors, and actual/game-displayed Insight differences accessible. Keep both Paper types on the main surface. Hole Gambit's whole condition now shares 12px text with its compact score and opens exact score details without splitting the sentence into a nested flex block.
- **Preservation:** The route wrapper, metadata, `useShallow`, `lastUpdated`, and completed-load guard remain unchanged. No domain/API, save, fixture, generated file, Part 2 surface, shared stack, or unrelated user change was edited. The original acceptance and correction records below remain historical.
- **Initial visual inspection and bounded fix:** Inspected desktop/narrow overview, compact Observations, Papers, and notes together. The initial native Anchor default appeared yellow; the final links use the existing brand-blue theme. The attempted sticky strip did not stay visible in the app's nested scroll layout, so it was replaced with explicit section return links. Narrow overview now uses two columns. The first full scenario stopped on a stale Paper assertion that counted a hidden exact-value duplicate; the assertion now counts only visible main text. No save or expected backend number changed.

#### Standards and maintainability

The [UI plan](research-part-1-ui-plan.md):113 also says no new formulas are required. Possible Duplicated Code and Feature Envy are supporting judgments, not separate findings. **Proposed correction:** Keep the grouped explanation and supplied component values without recomputing factor subtotals in the view. If numeric subtotals must remain, obtain approval for a narrow Research-owned display API that exposes named groups and calculated subtotals. Preserve all accepted calculations and the applied total; do not add a generic contributor framework.

### Fresh Phase 8 independent UI review — 2026-10-14

- **Authority and method:** The human requested an independent review of the selected Part 1 UI, including uncommitted work. Included the complete untracked `app/world-7/research/page.tsx` and `content.tsx`, navigation addition, and ten image-API lines in Research. #### Standards and maintainability

1. **R1 — P2: Converted source percentages disappear.** [Content](../../components/world-7/research/researchContent.tsx):47–55 excludes every `multiply` entry from input display and its exact tooltip. Accepted `researchPercentMultiplier` at [Research](../../data/domain/world-7/research.tsx):904–905 stores the percentage separately from its converted factor. Actual production shows Takin' Notes ×1.69 and Optimal Optometry ×1.35 without their 69% and 35% inputs. LANG-01 requires useful mechanic context. The [approved plan](research-part-1-ui-plan.md):49 also explicitly retains differing inputs that explain conversions. **Proposed correction:** Show the percentage input beside its converted factor, including exact detail and correct units. Distinguish these conversions from already-direct factors; do not label lens counts or base rates as percentages.
2. **R2 — P2: Arcade's description placeholder reaches player text.** [Content](../../components/world-7/research/researchContent.tsx):45–56 translates Papers and Gambit labels but passes Arcade's source unchanged. The actual EXP contributor capture reads `Arcade: +{% Research XP: Bonus +3.33%.` Its accepted source uses the Arcade effect template at [Research](../../data/domain/world-7/research.tsx):1002. This new presentation violates LANG-01's clear text requirement; approval does not excuse the template syntax. **Proposed correction:** Use the player-facing `Arcade: Research EXP` label while preserving its contribution and exact value. No calculation change or generated-data edit is needed.
3. **R3 — P3: The UI plan duplicates execution history.** [UI plan](research-part-1-ui-plan.md):123 records completed preview status, revision dates, latest-run dates, and historical proof state. PLAN-01 puts progress and execution history in the handoff. The duplicate record adds another place to keep current. **Proposed correction:** Keep an evergreen link to preview evidence in the plan. Leave completion states, dates, and run history in this handoff.

No grounded speculative abstraction, unnecessary framework, or impossible-missing-object safeguard was found in selected code. Local display helpers serve repeated current uses. Optional saved EXP requirements, save timestamps, and unplaced lens targets are genuine missing-data cases. The store starts with an empty domain map; completed-load readiness prevents reading unprepared singleton fields. Narrow image ownership, mutable-map refresh, shared rows, and ordinary navigation follow existing patterns.

#### Specification

1. **R4 — P2: Companion components lose their grouping.** [Content](../../components/world-7/research/researchContent.tsx):62–64 renders four flat `Bonus +…` children beneath `Companions: Multiplier ×6.21`. Actual and approved-preview multiplier captures both show this. Accepted [Research](../../data/domain/world-7/research.tsx):1011–1026 instead combines three factors: `(1 + Jellofish) × (1 + Rift Stalker + Rift Stalker level two) × (1 + Neonscale level two)`. Players cannot reconcile the flat additions with the displayed total. [Specification](research-spec-draft.md):75–76 requires additive versus multiplicative meaning; [UI plan](research-part-1-ui-plan.md):47 requires supplied contributor context. **Proposed correction:** Group or label the three factors and the Rift Stalker additions within their factor. Keep accepted calculations unchanged; no generic contributor framework is required.
2. **R1 also breaches the explicit conversion-context requirement** at UI-plan line 49. This is the same finding, not another defect. Correct final numeric outputs do not satisfy that display requirement.

No other concrete missing selected outcome or scope expansion was established. One read-only page keeps all Observations and notes visible by default. Search is the only feature control. Minimal Shape ownership and cumulative Point milestone context explain notes; no Part 2 balance, Grid catalogue, placement control, planner, forecast, or activity panel appears.

### Steering and its effect

The human clarified that Research level is account-wide and directed use of the highest saved character copy. This removed separate character progression and the unresolved level-selection choice. Stale copies until login remain a likely explanation, not a verified caller finding.

The human rejected invented upgrade families, default game-like boards, and cross-domain navigation. The outcome is a flat, list-first catalogue with name/effect search. Information stays visible by default; a Show unlocked checkbox is optional where useful.

The human kept related activities in separate workstreams and requested contributors beside their relevant Research stats. This removed activity progression panels and a standalone outside-bonuses catalogue. Research consumes required externally owned inputs without taking ownership of activity data.

The human selected current information only and made game code authoritative over descriptions and Wiki text. This excluded recommendations and simulations, settled code-backed Shape behavior, and moved non-material caller questions to later targeted investigation. Generic Observation “unavailable” wording became distinct level, discovery, attempt, and roll-threshold conditions.

## 1. What Research does

Research combines passive account growth with active discoveries and spending choices. Players unlock Observations, visit their maps, and click sparkling scenery. A limited daily roll can permanently discover an Observation. Placed lenses produce Research EXP or Insight. Research levels grant Points and milestones. Points buy Grid upgrades that improve Research or unlock and strengthen other activities. Shapes improve covered Grid upgrades. [P1, P2, G1–G4]

Start at the Research Desk in World 7 town, Shimmerfin Grove. Toobus Goobus gives the account-wide **RESEARCH STUDIES** quest. Its steps are to find an Observation, study it for EXP, and spend a Grid Point. It rewards two Half Finished Research Papers. The fetched quest text does not prove that preceding Spelunking quests strictly block desk access. [P1, P2]

The developer introduced Research as skill #20 on 2026-02-20. Online Research gains are 100%. Research AFK gains affect offline progress only. Online passive growth is not the same interaction as clicking scenery for discovery. Do not imply that players must remain at the Desk; that condition was not established. [P3]

Research level is account-wide. Research EXP grants are shared, while saves retain per-character level and EXP copies that can differ. [G5] Use the highest saved Research level across all characters. Human feedback identifies copies remaining stale until login as the likely explanation; the inspected callers did not newly verify that explanation. Do not show separate character progression or infer a maximum-EXP policy.

### Player loop

1. Place available lenses on discovered Observations.
2. Gain Research EXP and reach new level requirements.
3. Travel to an eligible Observation and spend an attempt at its sparkling scenery.
4. Reassign lenses between Research EXP, Insight, and adjacent-lens support.
5. Spend Grid Points on Research growth or linked-system rewards.
6. Move owned Shapes over useful Grid bonuses.
7. Use unlocked activities and their rewards to improve account progression.

The lens kinds are fixed for existing lens objects in the inspected allocation caller. “Reassign” means move an object, not freely change its kind. [G3]

## 2. Core aspects and player decisions

| Aspect | Mechanic and player interaction | Useful website outcome |
|---|---|---|
| Research level and EXP | Account-wide growth unlocks Observations, Points, lenses, Shapes, and capacity milestones. Saved character copies can differ. | Show the highest saved level across all characters. Do not infer which EXP copy represents account progress. [G1, G5, P1] |
| Observations | The current Wiki lists 43 named landmarks through level 100, with locations, hints, level requirements, and roll thresholds. | Show all entries in a list. Distinguish required level not met, already discovered, no remaining account attempts, and maximum roll below threshold. [P1, G2] |
| Discovery attempts | An account attempt permits a roll. Success requires a roll at or above the threshold; some thresholds exceed 100. | Show remaining attempts, daily allocation, current roll range, and failed-roll bonus. Level gating, exhausted allowance, and zero discovery chance are distinct facts. [G2] |
| Magnifying Glasses | Placed glasses produce Research EXP. Placement competes for limited lens objects and Observation capacity. | Show actual placements and current per-Observation contribution where verified. Do not label Insight lenses as Research EXP production. [G1, G3] |
| Optical Monocles and Insight | Monocles produce Insight, which levels individual Observations. N8 improves local Research EXP; O8 rewards total Observation levels. | Show each Observation's level, Insight progress, requirement, and effect. Distinguish local and total-level rewards. [G1, G3, P1] |
| Kaleidoscopes | Boost lenses on orthogonal neighboring Observation slots. Multiple neighbors add their contributions. | Show actual adjacency and current contributions beside affected EXP and Insight stats. Do not use diagonal adjacency or multiplicative neighbor stacking. [G3] |
| Grid Points | Earned Points include levels, milestone bonuses, and outside rewards. Each purchased level consumes a Point. | Show earned, spent, and available Points separately. A one-Point next level is not the full cost to reach a distant target. [G1, G4] |
| Grid upgrades | Spend Points on selectable squares. Owned orthogonal neighbors expose further squares. Each upgrade has its own maximum level. | Show purchased level, cap, current effect, reachability, and external requirements. Keep unlock flags and scaled bonuses distinct. [G1, G4] |
| Shapes | Move polygons over the Grid. Each square records one active Shape owner; overlap does not add bonuses. Rotation unlocks at level 90. | Show ownership, actual coverage, active Shape, and rotation requirement. Do not promise a universal strongest-Shape overlap rule. [G4] |
| Posty Notes | Fourteen level milestones explain extra lenses, Shapes, Points, capacity, and rotation. | Show reached and upcoming notes with their actual reward context. Outside Shape sources can change which Shape a note grants. [G1, P1] |
| Offline gains | Baseline offline fraction is 1%; the inspected AFK helper caps it at 100%. Offline effective time also feeds externally owned Minehead Currency. | Show current percentage and each supported source contribution beside it. Keep Minehead resource progression outside Research. [G6, P3] |
| Papers | Half Finished Research Papers grant two hours of progress, including Minehead Currency. Professional Papers are permanent Gem Shop AFK upgrades. | Distinguish consumable inventory from purchased permanent bonuses. Do not show historical Limited Specials as current stock. [P6] |

### Observations and roll semantics

Examples from the current Wiki include **Bored Tree** at level 1 in Valley Of The Beans and **Treeception** at level 52 in Hollowed Trunk. Later subjects include **Rifty Crystals** at level 94 in The Rift and **Happy Tree** at level 100 in Spore Meadows. The last listed thresholds reach 105. A placeholder named “Name” in a guide is not a verified additional Observation. The intended catalogue covers all 43 current named entries, not only these examples. [P1, P8]

Show discovery conditions separately. An unmet required level is a level gate. An already discovered Observation needs no further discovery. Zero remaining account attempts means the allowance is exhausted. A current maximum roll below the threshold means no roll in the current range can succeed. These facts can coexist. They do not establish map-access conditions. [G2]

The original roll caller uses inclusive minimum and maximum endpoints. Failure increments the shared failed-roll counter. Success clears that counter and marks the Observation found. Remaining attempts are separate from failures. The Smart Eye minimum increase uses its purchased level and the failure counter; its increase saturates after 25 failures in the inspected formula. [G2]

Daily restock **assigns** the current daily allowance to remaining attempts. It does not add unused attempts to that allowance. The inspected reset branch does not clear the failed-roll counter. Buying another K8 level immediately adds an attempt, so allocation minus remaining is not a reliable “attempts used today” value. The boundary follows the account's `ShopRestock` countdown, not a proved UTC-midnight reset. [G2]

### Lenses and Insight

The Observation board uses eight columns for adjacency. A Kaleidoscope affects left, right, above, and below; it excludes itself and diagonals. Adjacent Kaleidoscopes contribute through `1 + count × current base bonus`. This resolves the player-guide conflict about “either side” versus all four directions. [G3, P8]

Lens objects have saved position, target Observation, and kind. New objects receive required Monocle and Kaleidoscope allocations before ordinary Magnifying Glass allocation. The current per-Observation capacity reaches two at level 40, three at 70, and four at 120. Placing beyond capacity displaces another object. Moving lenses does not clear Insight levels in the inspected handler. [G1, G3]

Do not present Insight EXP as Research skill EXP. Do not promise a freely switchable lens mode. The inspected Insight accrual body processes one level-up per call rather than looping through every possible level. This limits claims about accrual behavior; away-time predictions are outside scope. [G3, G6]

### Shapes and Posty Notes

The current game defines ten Shapes: Purple Diamond, Blue Circle, Green Microsquare, Cyan Hexagon, Golden Rectangle, Orange Star, Maroon Triangle, White Moon, Pink Heart, and Bat Symbol. Use these definitions and the ownership helper's cap of ten. Wiki prose says eight, while its table lists ten. That discrepancy does not reopen the definition or ownership display choice. All-source obtainability was not exhaustively verified. [G1, G4, P1]

Posty Notes cover levels 10–140 in ten-level steps. They grant extra lenses at 10, 100, 130, and 140; Shapes at 20, 30, 50, 80, and 110; capacity at 40, 70, and 120; extra Points every ten levels from 60; and rotation at 90. Other Shape sources include Neonscale, Spelunking's Bah Dazzul, and Event Shop purchases. A note's pictured Shape need not match the next Shape received. [P1, P5]

**Evidence note:** Cyan Hexagon and Maroon Triangle descriptions promise Research EXP per highlighted bonus. Current `Grid_Bonus` code applies generic 20% and 25% modifiers. Use those code-backed modifiers for current effects. A Cyan-covered square produced the generic multiplier. No dedicated covered-square Research EXP contribution was established in inspected EXP/accrual paths. Game code determines behavior; descriptions and Wiki text do not override it. This discrepancy is not an approval blocker. [G4]

## 3. Grid upgrade catalogue and related activity boundaries

The current Wiki lists 60 named Grid upgrades. Use its flat per-upgrade catalogue as the coverage reference, with generated metadata for names, descriptions, caps, and base values. [P1, R3] Do not invent reward families or system categories. Search upgrade names and effects. Coordinates can identify game entries; they do not prescribe website layout.

Preserve each upgrade's name, purchased level, cap, current effect, unlock description, and useful adjacency or prerequisite information. Examples include Smart Eye's failed-roll minimum, Sharp Eye's maximum roll, Observationalistic's daily attempts, and Pts Every Ten's additional Points. Research EXP entries include Takin' Notes, All Night Studying, and See 'Em All. Powered Down Research and Research AFK Gains affect offline percentage. Holding Onto You, Kaleidoscope, Optical Monocle, Oracular Spectacular, Game Design 101, and Game Design 102 cover lenses, Insight, and Observation-level effects. These examples are coverage references, not categories. [P1, G1]

The same catalogue includes Research grants to Stickers, Polymerization, Tiny Cogs, Equinox, Gaming, Sailing, The Button, Sigils, Minehead, Legend Talents, Glimbo, Worship, Farming markets, Attire, Divinity, Jelly Operator, Sushi Station, Cooking Mastery, and Royal Guardian. Display each upgrade as its own entry. Preserve its actual effect and requirements, not an invented grouped inventory. [P1]

Retain specific effect context in individual entries. Masterclass Research affects Deathbringer, Windwalker, and Arcane Cultist, not Royal Guardian. Eclectic Sigils also requires Spelunking Lore Boss 7. Masterius Cookerius requires Rift 61. Revival of the Undead Battalion has a boat level 400 requirement. Divine Design has conditional Arctis/Doot multiplier behavior. These facts explain Research upgrades; they do not authorize downstream progress panels. [P1, P3, G1, G7]

Sources for catalogue coverage: current Wiki Grid [P1] and generated metadata [R3]. Current source confirms Minehead coordinate J6, selected unlock helpers, crown scaling, Glimbo blocks, and conditional multipliers [G1, G7]. Developer notes confirm K5 scope and recent Jelly entries [P3]. This is catalogue coverage, not exhaustive formula parity for all 60 entries.

### Related activities: separate ownership

Research owns its upgrade names, levels, caps, current effects, and unlock descriptions. Separate workstreams own related activity progression, resources, and actions. Research consumes existing externally owned values only when required for its effects or stat contributors.

- **Minehead:** Show Mr Minehead Linguistics and other Research upgrade effects. Minehead owns opponents, Currency, upgrades, attempts, and play state. J6 is code-backed; the Wiki introduction's J4 claim conflicts with it. [P4, G7]
- **Gaming Rat King:** Show Research unlock and crown-scaled effects. Consume the externally owned reclaimed-crown count when required for H9, I9, and H7. Gaming owns crown records, Garden, Palette, and Import Shop progression. [P1, G7]
- **Glimbo:** Show Research unlock and current effects. Consume existing total trades for I4's complete-100-trade blocks. Glimbo owns trade counts, materials, requirements, inventory, and affected Vault progression. J4's retroactive trade effect remains an upgrade description, not a trade panel. [P1, P8, G7]
- **Sushi Station:** Show Research unlock/effects and supported incoming Research contributions. Sushi owns Bucks, Fuel, Knowledge, Combo, tiers, and actions. The September patch's Combo reset and removed Salt Shaker contributions are historical source context only. [P3, P5, R2]
- **Jelly Operator:** Show H3/F3/G3 unlock/effects and supported incoming Research contributions. Jelly owns Cells, Bloodcells, operations, obstructions, upgrades, and actions. Launch totals do not audit current operation rules. [P3, R2]
- **The Button:** Show Research reward and skip upgrade effects. The Button owns requirements, skips, rewards, and independent progression. [P7, R2]

Apply the same ownership boundary to every related activity. Do not include full activity details, progress panels, opponent tables, currencies, tries, or trade inventories. Do not provide navigation links to other website domains. Source citations remain.

### Contributors beside Research stats

Show each supported source and its current contribution beside the Research stat it affects. Research EXP inputs include Farming, meals, Dancing Coral, Zenith Market, Gaming, Sailing, Tome, cards, Arcade, equipment sets, Spelunking, Equinox, Minehead/The Button, Killroy, companions, Sushi, The Hole, Jelly, and Royal Armory. Some inputs add percentages; others multiply. Do not sum description values into a false total. [G1]

Apply the same presentation to offline percentage, Insight and Kaleidoscope effects, Points, lens ownership, Shape ownership, and roll modifiers as appropriate. Relevant sources include Neonscale, Bah Dazzul, Jellofish, Rift Stalker, Paper Hardhat, Event Shop rewards, and Professional Papers. Show consumable Papers separately from permanent bonuses. Active pet bonuses can come from Pet Bonus Tokens without pet ownership. Historical catalogues do not establish current shop stock. Consume externally owned source values; do not create a standalone outside-bonuses catalogue or recommendation engine. [P3, P4–P6]

## 4. Proposed website outcomes and required interactions

This section records human direction for current information scope. It does not prescribe components or split delivery work. All website interactions are read-only. Travel, rolling, lens movement, Shape placement, purchases, trades, and activities occur in the game. [R4]

| Player question | Information the UI must provide | Website interaction |
|---|---|---|
| What is my current Research progress? | Highest saved account level, supported EXP context, save timestamp, discovery total, Observation levels, Points, lenses, Shapes, and milestones. | Inspect current overview and entries. Do not show separate character progression. |
| What can I discover? | All 43 named Observations, hints, maps, required levels, thresholds, and found state; current roll range and account attempts. | Browse list-first entries. Search by name or map. |
| Why can I not discover this Observation? | Required level not met, already discovered, no remaining account attempts, or current maximum roll below threshold. Multiple conditions can apply. | Read each distinct condition beside the entry. Do not infer map-access conditions. |
| What are my lenses doing? | Actual kinds and placements, capacity, Research EXP, Insight progress, and supported current source contributions and adjacency effects. | Inspect readable entries and actual neighbors. No hypothetical placement controls. |
| What do my Grid upgrades give me? | Earned/spent/available Points; upgrade names, levels/caps, current effects, unlock descriptions, reachability, and additional requirements. | Browse a list. Search upgrade names and effects. Inspect adjacency where useful. |
| What do my Shapes change? | Ten game definitions, owned Shapes, actual position/rotation, effective coverage, active owner, and code-backed current effects. | Inspect readable Shape and upgrade entries. Coordinates are optional references. |
| Which milestones have I reached? | All Posty Notes, thresholds, reached state, capacity, rotation, and ownership-sensitive Shape rewards. | Browse all milestones. |
| What discovery allowance remains? | Current account attempts and daily allowance; supported reset semantics. | Inspect current values. Do not derive used attempts from allowance minus remaining. |
| What does a related unlock give me? | Research upgrade state, effect, unlock description, and prerequisites such as Lore Boss 7 or Rift 61. | Inspect Research entries only. No cross-domain navigation or activity detail panels. |
| How does offline progress differ? | Current percentage, baseline, cap, and supported source contributions beside the stat. Online gains are 100%. | Read current rate context. No forecasts or ETA planning. |

Use list-first upgrades and Observations. Do not recreate the Grid or Observation board by default or force spatial layouts. Preserve useful actual coverage, effects, and adjacency in entries. Exclude simulators, optimizers, recommendations, hypothetical comparisons, discovery probabilities, and ETA planning. Verified current rates and effects remain in scope.

Show all information by default. An optional **Show unlocked** checkbox can narrow a list where useful. There is no pending concealment or spoiler choice.

Descriptions belong beside related entries. The page should explain unfamiliar terms without becoming a separate mechanics guide. Missing parsing does not reduce the proposed whole-feature information scope.

## 5. Supported inputs and current coverage limits

|---|---|
| Grid catalogue | Generated metadata supplies indices, names, descriptions, caps, and base bonus values. The repository spans 240 positions, including placeholder `Name` cells. Positions are not 240 real upgrades. [R1, R3] |
| Purchased levels and active Shape per square | Existing domain parses these and computes ordinary/specialized bonuses. It does not expose a full Research player surface. [R1] |
| Discovery flags and Insight levels | Existing parser reads them for selected derived Grid bonuses. That does not provide an initialized full Observation catalogue, placement view, or daily-action model. [R1] |
| Lenses, Shape positions, counters, and Insight progress | Current original-game callers establish these state families. The existing domain does not model them as complete UI-facing concepts. Static-data availability needs a targeted audit; this brief does not declare every missing domain field a missing export. [G2–G4, R1] |
| Research level and EXP | Shared grants and divergent saved copies are verified. Use the highest saved level across characters, as directed. The revised plan supplies saved EXP with source-character provenance and its own saved requirement. No maximum-EXP policy follows. [G5, E1] |
| Rat King crowns and Glimbo trades | Existing Research code parses aggregate inputs for affected bonuses. Those values belong to separate activities; shared storage does not transfer ownership. Research consumes existing external values only as needed. [G7, R1] |
| Rates and timers | Source callers now establish hourly Research EXP and Insight, direct producer selectors, and the Research-specific AFK branch. The Insight UI and accrual use different multipliers. Website parity, full claim behavior, cache lifecycle, and save-age handling remain unverified. Only supported current rates and effects belong in scope. [E2–E7] |
| Other activity pages | Existing World 7 surfaces include Sushi, Jelly Operator, Spelunking, Dancing Coral, and Legend Talents. Separate workstreams own their progression and actions. This footprint does not authorize Research detail panels, redesigns, or navigation links. [R2] |

### Raw mapping ledger

Raw identifiers belong here as historical source-boundary evidence, not player-facing terminology. A shared save array does not assign domain ownership. Minehead state, crowns, and Glimbo records remain externally owned even when stored in `Research`. [G2–G7, R1]

- `Research[0]`: purchased Grid levels; `[1]`: active Shape owner per square; `[2]`: found Observation flags.
- `Research[3]`: partial Insight EXP per Observation; `[4]`: Insight levels; `[5]`: lens quadruplets `(x, y, target Observation, kind)`. Target `-1` means unplaced. Kinds `0/1/2` mean Magnifying Glass/Optical Monocle/Kaleidoscope. [E2, E3]
- `Research[6]`: Shape position/rotation quadruplets; the fourth field remains unresolved.
- `Research[7][2]`: remaining discovery attempts; `[7][3]`: accumulated effective progress seconds; `[7][8]`: remaining Minehead attempts.
- `Research[8]`: Minehead upgrades; `[11]`: unique reclaimed crown records; `[12]`: Glimbo counts by trade.
- Runtime `OptionsListAccount` is saved as `OptLacc`. Index 514 is failed-roll count; 512 tracks tutorial actions. These are not daily used-roll totals.
- Character `Lv0_<id>[20]`, `Exp0_<id>[20]`, and `ExpReq0_<id>[20]`: saved Research level, EXP, and requirement copies. `Player.skills` exposes each copy as `SkillData`. The raw startup `Research` field was a JSON string; runtime exposes its decoded array. [E1]

The documented offline service was ready. Queries returned **Jelly_Operator v1.22** and identified `ResearchStuff` in `ActorEvents_579`. Current source accessors and safe calculation queries ran. No action method ran. Original helpers can update internal calculation caches. The instance was warmed before inspection, so its runtime state must not be presented as an untouched save baseline. [O1, G1–G8]

| Observed value | Result | Evidence meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| Selected character index | 3 | Offline instance selection, not cloud-save active-character proof. |
| Selected character's Research level copy | 70 | Observed copy, not separate character progression or an account EXP policy. |

| Lens ownership / per-Observation capacity | 14 / 3 | Current helper results. |
| Optical Monocles / Kaleidoscopes | 4 / 4 | Fixed-kind allocations in this state. |
| Shape ownership | 5 | Not proof of maximum obtainable count. |

The same startup save contained Research level copies `[69,69,68,70,68,69,68,68,69,69,69]`. The directed highest-saved-level rule yields 70 for that save. Current runtime EXP differed from startup input before inspection; no cause was established. Human feedback gives stale copies until login as the likely level-copy explanation, not a newly verified caller fact. No maximum-EXP rule was established. [G5]

Source inspection proves the roll comparison and inclusive endpoints. No roll was executed. Source inspection proves four-neighbor Kaleidoscope support and additive stacking; current helper outputs also matched two-neighbor multipliers. No lens or Shape drag was exercised. No live online callback, daily boundary crossing, paper use, reset, or complete linked minigame run was exercised.

Closed correctness work is historical context only. Issue #386 was accepted through a human-directed exception with narrowed square-94 scope. Its closeout does not record final passing domain tests or independent review. It does not certify the entire Grid. [R5]

## 7. Recorded human direction

1. Research level is account-wide. Use the highest saved level across all characters; do not infer maximum EXP.
2. Use a flat upgrade catalogue and list-first Observations. Search upgrade names and effects, not invented categories.
3. Show distinct Observation conditions: unmet level, already discovered, exhausted account attempts, and maximum roll below threshold.
4. Keep all information visible by default. An optional Show unlocked checkbox can narrow useful lists.
5. Show supported current contributors beside relevant Research stats, not in a standalone outside-bonuses catalogue.
6. Keep related activity data and actions externally owned. Research shows its upgrades and consumes required existing source values only.
7. Exclude cross-domain navigation, activity detail panels, spatial UI mandates, hypothetical placements, simulations, recommendations, optimizers, probabilities, and ETA planning.
8. Use code-backed Cyan/Maroon generic modifiers and the ten current Shape definitions with ownership cap ten. Description discrepancies are evidence notes.

The human approved these decisions and this brief on 2026-10-13. Approval does not authorize delivery planning or implementation. Source citations remain.

## 8. Remaining factual limits

The Phase 3 revision resolves source-level units, Insight progress, lens encoding, direct Research AFK inputs, and contributor ownership. Saved EXP still has no source-proved account-authoritative copy. The approved display policy uses one provenance-labeled saved copy without changing the highest-saved-level direction. The blanket static-export blocker is superseded by the [source-data boundary](#source-data-and-calculation-boundary). Paired runtime, core-output, and direct-parameter proof remain pending. See [revision evidence](#part-1-planning-revision-evidence).

Other questions can receive targeted source or caller research later. They do not block approval of this information scope:

- Shape refresh and overlap callers do not establish a universal strongest-Shape rule. Show supported actual ownership and coverage only.
- A player-accessible Grid reset/refund was not found in the bounded inspection. Absence was not proved.
- Daily-restock origin, timezone, and save-age behavior are not settled. Do not invent UTC-midnight timers.
- Full online callbacks, large away-time claims, repeated Insight level-ups, Papers, and event time grants were not exhaustively traced.
- Observation hints, thresholds, lens kinds, Shape definitions, and note descriptions are available in generated exports. The [source-data boundary](#source-data-and-calculation-boundary) separates catalogue inputs from handwritten mechanics.
- Current Wiki states no Research Rift Skill Mastery in build 1.22. Old snippets are insufficient to add a mastery display. [P1]

Related activity rates, currencies, costs, tries, and reset rules belong to their workstreams, not a broader Research option. These evidence limits do not reopen approved product choices or authorize placeholder facts. Targeted evidence and static-data audits belong inside the relevant proposed part after its next phase is authorized.

## Part 1 planning revision evidence

The earlier source revision inspected source bodies only. It did not call their calculation or action bodies. The offline `/status` identified an isolated, ready instance at character `3`, not an account-active-character declaration. Source retrieval used the documented `/exec` endpoint with `.toString()` expressions. The original v1.22 helper capture remains a durable formula reference. Temporary source artifacts are inspection evidence, not fixtures or supported static exports. The current data-boundary revision reused this evidence and read existing export code; it made no new runtime request.

### Source and caller findings

- **E1 — Saved EXP provenance:** [Player skill data](../../data/domain/player.tsx):91–93,766,776,887–894 exposes `SkillData(level, currentXP, xpReq)` from each character's `Lv0_`, `Exp0_`, and `ExpReq0_` arrays. [Existing Research parsing](../../data/domain/world-7/research.tsx):321–350 already selects the highest level for eligible Observations. G5 establishes shared grants and divergent saved copies; it does not prove an account-authoritative EXP copy. The plan's lowest-ID highest-level reference is an explicit presentation proposal, not a source-discovered game rule. It retains the selected copy's name, level, EXP, and requirement.
- **E2 — Insight progress, requirements, and units:** `ActorEvents_741.prototype._customEvent_ResearchStuffs.toString()`, token `g`, adds `ObservationInsightExpRate(t) × Research[7][3] / 3600` to `Research[3][t]`. It subtracts one requirement and increments `Research[4][t]` once when that requirement is met. It grants `ResearchEXPrateTOT × Research[7][3] / 3600` to skill `20`. [Research helper](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):74–120 supplies hourly base EXP, Insight, requirements, and total EXP. Requirement is `(2 + 0.7t) × (1.75 + t/200)^InsightLevel × (1 + t²/100) + InsightLevel`. Pending seconds are not partial Insight EXP. Inspected source artifact: `local://research-revision-researchCaller.txt`.
- **E3 — Display discrepancy and actual placement:** `ActorEvents_741.prototype._event_2.toString()` renders EXP with `XP]hr` and Insight with `]HR`. Its Insight figure is `ObservationInsightExpRate × ResearchEXPmulti`; token `g` accrual omits that second multiplier. This discrepancy must remain visible rather than silently changing the formula. `_event_.toString()` writes lens offsets `0/1` as pixel `x/y`, offset `2` as the Observation index, and retains kind at offset `3`. Capacity displacement writes target `-1`. `_customEvent_ResearchStuffs`, token `e`, derives displayed positions with target remainder/division by `8`; token `h` allocates fixed kinds. Artifacts: `local://research-revision-741-methods.json`, `local://research-revision-researchDrawing.txt`, and E2's caller artifact.
- **E4 — Explicit Research offline branch:** `ActorEvents_124._customBlock_AFKgainrates.toString()`, branch `"Research"`, returns `max(0.01, min(1, 0.01 + sum/100))`. The sum contains companion `28`, Hole Gambit `15`, Minehead rewards `1/10`, Grid `71/111`, `min(6, round(Sailing[3][36]))`, `min(CardLv("w7b11"), 10)`, `20 × Companions(153) + 10 × CompLV2(153)`, `2 × GemItemsPurchased[45]`, and Sushi rewards `4/24`. Generic skilling AFK additions do not feed this branch. `ActorEvents_189._customBlock_AFKcode.toString()` adds offline seconds multiplied by the Research fraction to pending `Research[7][3]`. Artifacts: `local://research-revision-offline.txt` and `local://research-revision-afkcode.txt`. [Research helper](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):9 converts the fraction to percent.
- **E5 — Character-sensitive inputs:** Current `ActorEvents_12._customBlock_RunCodeOfTypeXforThingY.toString()`, selector `CardLv`, uses account card counts, not equipped-card effects. [Card.getLevels](../../data/domain/cards.tsx):68–82 provides native progression. `ActorEvents_579._customBlock_GetSetBonus.toString()` checks permanent activation in account option `379`, otherwise current-character `EquipmentOrder[0]` armor and `[1]` tools plus the set's special-piece rule. These indices are equipment categories, not character IDs. [EquipmentSets](../../data/domain/misc/equipmentSets.tsx):77–96 has an unfinished equipped-player branch. `ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Thingies.toString()`, `LoreEpiBon`, reads Spelunking epilogue ownership and current-character Tome score, Grimoire, and Troll-set effects. It is not the website Tome epilogue getter. Artifacts: `local://research-revision-_customBlock_RunCodeOfTypeXforThingY.txt`, `local://research-revision-sets.txt`, and `local://research-revision-_customBlock_Thingies.txt`.
  - Tome score provenance is reproducible through `ActorEvents_229.prototype._customEvent_TomeQTY.toString()`. It sets `_GenInfo[84]` from the current cached `DNSM.TomeQTYtot`, whose components include account aggregates and current-context values. The Spelunking owner must consume that source-context total, not assume the website's account-best Tome score is equivalent. Source-only artifact: `local://research-revision-tome-score-context.json`.
- **E6 — Direct selector equivalence:** Current `ActorEvents_579` helper sources confirm Minehead `BonusQTY` means reached-opponent rewards; Sushi `RoG_BonusQTY` means discovered-Sushi rewards; `ActorEvents_189._customBlock_JellyOperation`, `RoG_BonusQTY`, means completed-obstruction rewards. The original Minehead helper's `Button_Bonuses` belongs to the website Button producer. `GamingStatType("MSA_Bonus",10)` uses Superbit `44` and complete item-count blocks above `300`, not Palette. `Sailing("SlabboBonus",7)` uses Superbit `34`, Lab `15`, Slab scaling, and complete item-count blocks above `1300`, not an artifact getter. `Holes` forwards unhandled selectors to `Holes2`; `GambitBonuses(15)` has score and tutorial-scene gates. `Cglunko_upgBon(11)` uses account option `641` and the source's upgrade base value. `RandomEvent("KillroyBonuses",5)` returns `1 + OptLacc[469]/(150 + OptLacc[469]) × 0.8`; Research then applies `/100`. Preserve that normalization. Artifacts follow `local://research-revision-_customBlock_<helper>.txt` for `Minehead`, `SushiStuff`, `GamingStatType`, `Sailing`, `Holes`, `Holes2`, `RandomEvent`, and `JellyOperation`.
- **E7 — API and phase evidence:** The [plan's producer contract](research-part-1-backend-plan.md#direct-producer-api-and-readiness-contract) records each direct owner/API and required additions. Exact current registration is [idleonData.tsx](../../data/domain/idleonData.tsx):271–395. Early Grid is line `282`; Farming stickers `284`; Button `285`; Gaming `293`; cards `313`; Sailing artifact/Slab preparation `320`; Tome scores/bonuses `325/329`; Hole `326`; Sneaking `330`; Crop Scientist `331`; Arcade/Cooking/Sailing `337/338/340`; Sushi `348`; players `349`; late Royal Armory `391`. New UI-only Research preparation follows these settled inputs. Missing equipped-set, Spelunking epilogue, MSA, Slabbo, Cglunko, active-token, and selected-Outpost results are named owner work, not permission for Research to duplicate them. Public API evidence:
  - [Farming](../../data/domain/world-6/farming.tsx):430–431,455–463,559,696–698; [Cooking](../../data/domain/world-4/cooking.tsx):519–521; [Dancing Coral](../../data/domain/world-7/dancingCoral.tsx):77–79; [Zenith Market](../../data/domain/world-7/zenithShop.tsx):73–75.
  - [Cards](../../data/domain/cards.tsx):68–82; [Spelunking](../../data/domain/world-7/spelunking.tsx):181–184,238–240; [EquipmentSets](../../data/domain/misc/equipmentSets.tsx):77–96; [Tome score/epilogue context](../../data/domain/world-4/tome.tsx):394–404.
  - [Minehead](../../data/domain/world-7/minehead.tsx):120–125; [Button](../../data/domain/world-7/button.tsx):75–77; [Sushi](../../data/domain/world-7/sushi.tsx):308–313; [Jelly](../../data/domain/world-7/jellyOperator.tsx):279–286.
  - [Gambit](../../data/domain/world-5/hole/gambit.tsx):111–119; [Fountain](../../data/domain/world-5/hole/fountain.tsx):42–44; [Sneaking](../../data/domain/world-6/sneaking.tsx):470–476; [Sailing artifact parsing](../../data/domain/world-5/sailing/sailing.tsx):335–339.

- **E8 — Ownership-sensitive notes:** [Research helper](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):206–219 caps Shape ownership at ten, adds ordinary level gates `20/30/50/80/110`, Event Shop `36`, and level-20-gated companion `54` and Spelunking Lore `7`. `ActorEvents_741.prototype._event_Research.toString()` displays Shape index `i` only while `i < ShapesOwned`, confirming the current ordered ownership prefix. That does not prove which past source granted a Shape or promise an upcoming pictured Shape. Posty Notes are reached by level, not claimed flags. The Points-per-ten-level term at helper:229–230 gains its extra level-60 Point for every reached ten-level block and also uses purchased Grid `50`. No Point balance or Shape-coverage surface moves into Part 1.

### Source-data and calculation boundary

The human's clarification permits initially handwritten verified metadata as well as stable rules beside their responsible calculations. Reuse existing exports. Missing numeric fields and catalogue location alone do not require an export or create a delivery blocker. A new export requires a concrete current maintenance reason. Current [Research metadata](../../data/domain/data/ResearchMetadataRepo.ts):9–15 already supplies roll bounds, allocation, Kaleidoscope base boost, and rotation. The [Observation model](../../data/domain/model/researchObservationModel.ts):3–12 supplies discovery and location records. Neither schema needs production or Insight coefficients.

| Verified mechanic | Website owner and source evidence |
|---|---|
| Eight-column adjacency | Research's support calculation uses one named column constant. [Research helper](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):169–202 and E3 establish row edges and orthogonal neighbors. Pixel positions do not define adjacency. |
| Ownership and capacity | Research keeps the base counts, caps `80/4/10`, and verified milestone associations beside those calculations. Helper:51–73,206–218 establishes the rule. Reuse exported note thresholds; reward prose is not a numeric parser. |
| Observation EXP curve | Observation calculates its index-based base production from helper:74–92. The source defines a formula, not a stored per-Observation production catalogue. Keep coefficients in that calculation; do not generate a table of formula outputs. |
| Insight and Kaleidoscope | Observation owns Insight production and requirements; Research owns shared support. Helper:93–113,162–168 and E2 establish the coefficients and Emporium conversions. There is no supported Insight cap. |
| Shared EXP conversions | Research keeps its direct card/set caps and companion conversions beside the multiplier. Equinox owns its level-based contribution. Helper:122–161 establishes the source formula. Consume existing producer metadata and results for foreign rewards. |
| Research offline rule | Research owns its baseline, floor, cap, and direct conversion coefficients. E4 establishes the exact branch. Generic skilling AFK formulas do not replace it. |

Existing exports also supply three lens definitions, 14 Posty Notes, and ten Shapes. [Paper item metadata](../../data/domain/data/ItemDetailRepo.ts):34157–34162 supplies `Quest115`. [Professional Papers](../../data/domain/data/GemShopRepo.ts):1701–1711 supplies purchase `45`, description, price, and purchase cap. The two-per-purchase AFK conversion remains a verified Research formula term, not a reason to parse that description or add metadata.

Required producer API additions remain website implementation on their existing owners. A missing calculated output does not imply missing static data. Verified unexported metadata can initially remain beside its handwritten owner, as the Hole value below does. No automatic field/export blocker remains; a new export requires a concrete current maintenance reason.

### Hole upgrade base reward evidence

**Verified named value:** `holeResearchExpUpgradeBasePercent = 1`. Static reading of `../IdleonWikiBot/codefiles/idleon1.22.txt`:307010–307039 establishes that `RandoListo2` returns 15 string-split rows. Name row `11` at line 307031 identifies `Researchy` at zero-based index `11`. Reward row `13` at line 307037 contains the source literal `"1 15 2 8 3 1 1 3 1 10 1 1 25 3 10 100 10 25 2 1 5 150 2 1"`. Its zero-based entry `11` is `1`, not `25`.

Static parsing also finds row `13`, index `11` equal to `1` in retained sibling captures `codefiles/idleon1.21.txt`:305139, `codefiles/idleon1.20c.txt`:304994, and `codefiles/idleon1.19.txt`:300109. These captures show no per-field value churn for this reward. They do not prove future stability.

E6's source artifact, `local://research-revision-_customBlock_Holes2.txt`, contains the selector body: `Cglunko_upgBon(t) = OptionsListAccount[round(630 + t)] * CustomLists.RandoListo2[13][t]`. At `t = 11`, ownership comes from account option `641`. The owned level multiplied by `1` returns one percent per owned level. [Research helper](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):154 converts that result to the separate factor `1 + Hole result / 100`. Hole parses named ownership, keeps the named value beside its calculation, and exposes its calculated public result to Research.

The existing source-capture path is sibling WikiBot `helpers/CodeReader.py`:195. It registers `RandoListo2 = function ()` under the section name `GamingMetadata`. Current supported consumers omit reward row `13`:

- `repositories/misc/world5/GamingMetadataRepo.py`:20–60 parses plant luck, log ranks, and rat-shop labels only. Its model, `definitions/misc/world5/GamingMetadata.py`:25–28, declares only those three fields. The [website model](../../data/domain/model/gamingMetadataModel.ts):5–8 matches them.
- `repositories/misc/world5/GamingImportRepo.py`:18–32 uses rows `1/2/4/5`.
- `repositories/misc/world5/GamingSpeciesRepo.py`:18–42 uses row `0`.

These paths are relative to sibling `../IdleonWikiBot`. They establish export omission only, not meaningful patch churn or a requirement for a new export. The raw source section's Gaming name does not transfer Hole's calculation ownership.

No concrete current maintenance reason requires WikiBot for this value. Keep only `holeResearchExpUpgradeBasePercent = 1` beside Hole's handwritten calculation; do not duplicate the reward table. Cost row `14`, full upgrade tables, and formula exports are unnecessary. Existing exports remain the source for records they already provide. New exports require a concrete current maintenance reason. No WikiBot edits, generation, or sync ran. Saved same-copy `xpReq` needs no new requirement export.

## Sources

### Current original-game evidence

Inspected on 2026-10-12 through the read-only expression workflow documented in [O1]. Accessor names below reproduce source lookup without relying on temporary agent artifacts. Source bodies were inspected; action bodies were not invoked.

- **O1:** [Offline tool README](../../sub-projects/idleon-offline/README.md):3,35–65. The isolated tool runs original assets, pauses time between requests, and exposes `/status`, `/exec`, and startup `/cloud-save`. It is not a connected live game.
- **G1:** `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_ResearchStuff.toString()`. Current v1.22 helper. Branches: `OccurrencesToBeFound`, roll limits, lens counts, EXP/Insight rates, `Kalei_MultiTot`, Points, Shapes, Grid selection/bonuses, and linked unlocks. Durable historical comparison: [ResearchStuff v1.22 capture](../game-snippets/research/ResearchStuff.1.22.js):8–120,122–219,220–284,318–348. The capture dates from 2026-09-19; fresh source was also queried this session.
- **G2:** `ActorEvents_672.prototype._event_Release` and `_event_Drawing`; `com.stencyl.behavior.Script.randomInt`; `scripts.Design_23_23_EveryScene.prototype._event_Every1sec`. Accessed through `idleon.allScripts` or `window.frames[0].__idleon_cheats__`. Roll threshold, inclusive endpoints, remaining attempts, failure reset, and daily `ShopRestock` refill. `ActorEvents_741.prototype._event_` adds an attempt when square 90 is upgraded.
- **G3:** `ActorEvents_741.prototype._customEvent_ResearchStuffs`, tokens `h`, `e`, and `g`; `_event_`; G1 lens/Insight/Kaleidoscope branches. Saved lens kinds, allocation, displacement, adjacency, and progress accrual.
- **G4:** `ActorEvents_741.prototype._event_`, `_event_2`, and `_customEvent_ResearchStuffs`, token `f`; G1 Shape/selection/bonus branches; `CustomLists.Research` and `ResGridSquares`. Shape geometry/ownership, rotation, overlap gates, current descriptions, coordinates, and executed Cyan bonus example.
- **G5:** `ActorEvents_266._customBlock_giveEXP`, `_customBlock_CloudSAVE`, and `_customBlock_initialize3`; `ActorEvents_29.prototype._event_LevelUps`; startup `/cloud-save` and current selected level/EXP reads. Shared grants, initial Research setup, serialization, and divergent character copies.
- **G6:** `ActorEvents_124._customBlock_AFKgainrates`, Research branch; `ActorEvents_189._customBlock_AFKcode`; G3 token `g`. Offline baseline/cap, accumulated effective seconds, and EXP/Insight/Minehead accrual. Full online callback path remains unverified.
- **G7:** `ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Minehead`; `ActorEvents_577.prototype._customEvent_GardenETC`; `ActorEvents_741.prototype._event_11`; G1 secondary Grid modes. Minehead stages/Currency, unique crown collection, Glimbo trade records, and complete-100-trade scaling.
- **G8:** Executed v1.22 helper packet: Points, roll limits/allocation, lens ownership/capacity/kinds, Shapes, AFK percentage, selected level, crowns, and Glimbo count. Observed results are retained in section 6. They belong to the warmed offline instance, not a same-state live/website parity test.
